# Verification Record

## Current phase

Implementation is active. OpenSpec records and plugin test/support sources are
advancing together. The user-directed cadence remains in force: complete a
coherent source stage, build it once, batch compile/runtime repairs, and only
then run the broad prefix. UE build and Automation evidence now exists for the
implemented subset, and method/API/internal/predecessor inventories now have
terminal dispositions. Per-product assertion-depth review, semantic file
splits, linked production-repair reconciliation, and final environment gates
remain open. Therefore a green active prefix is not represented as complete
coverage.

## Implementation verification contract

Exact future commands and order are in tasks 18–21. The important sequencing rule is:

1. write all planned support/tests/migrations;
2. complete static catalog/source/API/format/boundary reconciliation;
3. run the first planned integration build;
4. batch compile repairs;
5. run focused theme/domain prefixes and batch runtime repairs;
6. run full SDK, `NativeCore`, and `All`;
7. regenerate evidence, update documentation, and perform final scoped review.

No unexecuted future command is represented as passing in this record.

The three initial red discoveries are preserved in `issues.md`: the fork
rejects mutable script globals, raw native callbacks require an explicit
`ASAutoCaller` caller in this build, and a generated `for` variable condition
must advance its local state to avoid an infinite loop. All 180 cells remain
enabled after those corrections; no semantic direction was deleted or turned
into a representative smoke test.
